Purpose
This block inventories existing Supabase client/server-only/admin patterns and creates the implementation plan for future read-only app user mapping.

This block is inventory/report-only.
It does not implement a DB read.
It does not implement a DB write.
It does not execute SQL.
It does not call AI.
It does not create the future mapping diagnostic endpoint.
It does not create a Supabase admin client.
It does not modify src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts.
It does not insert activity_events.
It does not persist semantic candidates, Value Objects, state facts, state deltas, or state snapshots.

O-CF schema decisions carried forward
- Exact app user table: public.app_users.
- Exact internal app user id field: public.app_users.id.
- Exact Auth0 subject field: public.app_users.auth0_sub.
- Lookup predicate: public.app_users.auth0_sub equals trusted server-side Auth0 authSubject.
- Provider must be enforced from trusted Auth0 session, not from public.app_users.
- public.app_users has UNIQUE (auth0_sub).
- app_user_inactive is not supported by current public.app_users schema.
- RLS is enabled for public.app_users.
- No RLS policies were returned for public.app_users.
- Future read-only mapping needs server-only Supabase service-role/admin access.

- nextStepDecision: Proceed to O-CH server-only admin client contract or read-only mapping implementation contract, depending on inventory review.

Future read-only mapping implementation plan
The future implementation corridor should be split into separate gates:
1. Server-only Supabase admin client contract.
2. Server-only Supabase admin client implementation or reuse proof.
3. Read-only app user mapping helper implementation.
4. Diagnostic endpoint implementation for /api/activity/debug/server-side-app-user-mapping-read-only-test.
5. Browser-authenticated runtime verification of mapped_user_found and safe failure cases.
6. No-write controlled-intake authenticated preview gate.
7. Only later: controlled-intake route integration.
8. Only later: activity_events insert gate.

Required future mapping behavior
- Read trusted server-side Auth0 session only after diagnostic header passes.
- Accept only provider auth0.
- Require trusted authSubject.
- Query public.app_users by auth0_sub equals trusted authSubject.
- Return mapped_user_found if exactly one app user is found.
- Return app_user_not_found if zero app users are found.
- Fail closed if more than one row is ever returned defensively, even though UNIQUE (auth0_sub) exists.
- Do not execute inactive check because current schema has no active/status field.
- Report appUserInactiveSupported false.
- Report appUserInactiveCheckExecuted false.

Safe response boundary
- Do not return raw DB rows.
- Do not return raw Auth0 session.
- Do not return tokens.
- Do not return cookies.
- Do not return full provider payload.
- Do not return full user record.
- Default proof response should use booleans and safe statuses.
- appUserId may be exposed only if a later contract explicitly allows a sanitized proof field.

Implementation remains forbidden in O-CG
O-CG does not authorize code implementation.
O-CG authorizes only the next contract/planning step.

Next step decision
Proceed to O-CH.
O-CH should create the server-only Supabase admin client contract before read-only mapping implementation.
O-CH must remain no DB read, no DB write, no SQL, no AI, no diagnostic endpoint creation and no controlled-intake route integration.

PASS marker
PASS_SERVER_ONLY_SUPABASE_CLIENT_INVENTORY_AND_MAPPING_PLAN_CREATED_NO_DB_NO_WRITE_NO_SQL_NO_AI
